# Pose filter tuning grid (ONY-502)

Generated by `dart run tool/pose_filter_tune.dart`. 6 captures.

Normative spec: `filter-spec` rev 4, section 7. Grid: minCutoff 1.0-4.0 Hz step 0.25, beta 0.00-0.40 Hz per cm/s step 0.01, dCutoff 0.5-4.0 Hz step 0.5. 4264 points per runtime, all replayed; no early stop and no pruning.

Bars: J <= 0.5 mm, J_D <= 0.75 mm, median L <= 20.0 ms on medium and fast sweeps, median L <= 160.0 ms on slow sweeps (regression only). Selected point: lowest J_D, then lowest median L.

A winner on a bar-limiting boundary is inconclusive. The tool extends that axis 50 % at the same step and reruns the whole grid.
